M
Update
Unimicron  | Asia Pacific
Investigation relating to trans-
shipping China-made PCB
What happened? On August 28, the Taoyuan District Prosecutors Office directed
investigators to visit Unimicron to conduct a search and investigation, primarily to
examine matters relating to Offenses Against Agriculture, Industry, and Commerce.
• According to local media (Taipei Times, 30 Aug 2026), five defendants were
released on bail ranging from NT$0.3M to NT$15M, and nine were released

without bail.
Unimicron's official response: Unimicron announced it will fully cooperate with the
investigative authorities. The matter primarily concerns a dispute over trans-
shipping China-made boards to Taiwan for "Made in Taiwan" relabeling under the
offense of obstructing agriculture, industry, and commerce. The individuals involved
in the investigation include one general manager, one deputy general manager, and
colleagues from the relevant departments. There is no material impact seen on the

company's financial condition or business operations, but Unimicron said it will issue
a material information announcement after the District Prosecutors Office provides
a formal explanation.

Key Takeaways
Limited fundamental impact to numbers and our positive view. We would view
any pullback as a good opportunity to accumulate, especially if below NT$950.
Our OW thesis on Unimicron is centered on its ABF story, and this investigation is
related to its Hejiang site, which produces PCBs (2025 Annual Report).
PCB (including HDI) was 36% of total revenue in 2Q26, but lower in terms of

profits.
The Hejiang factory that is under investigation was established in 1996-97, which
seems to imply PCBs produced there are more likely non-AI related PCBs.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 15%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.

Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers
n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Unimicron
Valuation
Our target price for Unimicron is NT$1,500. We use a 1-year forward-looking DCF-based valuation (ie, end-2027) to reflect
Unimicron's ability to generate stable cash. With a risk-free rate of 4.3% (10-year government bond rate), a market risk
premium of 7%, and an equity beta of 1.43 (2-year weekly share price), we calculate Unimicron's WACC as 13.7%. Our target
price is equivalent to 66x/31x 2026/27E EPS, which is higher than the +1std level of consensus PE multiple of the past five

years. However, as the market focuses on 2027 upcycle, we believe valuation on 2027E EPS is still in line with historical peak
valuations of 25–30x during the last upcycles.
Risks
Key downside risks that could mean Unimicron shares trade below our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected HDI
business; 2) significant increase in ABF supply in the industry; and 3) stronger-than-expected margin pressure from
depreciation due to higher capex investments.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Update
AirTAC International  | Asia Pacific
August Sales -7% MoM/+43%
YoY
Here we focus on AirTAC's monthly sales, which we believe
could be a catalyst for its share price.
What's new:
• August sales came in at NT$3,708mn (-7% MoM/+43% YoY), with the
sequential decline mainly due to seasonality.
• By segment, shipments of textiles (+47% YoY), electronics (+36% YoY),
machine tool (+35% YoY) and battery (+30% YoY) outperformed during the
month.
• QTD revenue reached 66% of our estimate at NT$11,586mn (-5% QoQ/+41%

YoY) and 70% of consensus' at NT$11,015mn (-9% QoQ/+34% YoY).
Outlook:
• We see its 3Q26 revenue  tracking in line with our estimate.
• Management remains positive on its growth outlook driven by its share gains
and the policy-supported industry upcycle, which it expects to extend into
2027.
• Stay OW, as current valuation of 20x 2027e P/E is undemanding vs. its mid-
cycle average of 25x.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 29x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). As the industry enters an up-cycle, we think applying a near-peak cycle
valuation of 29x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 2

For the month of September 2026, we estimate AirTAC’s monthly revenue will increase 
to Rmb827mn (+6% MoM, +17% YoY)/NT$3,930mn (+6% MoM, +27% YoY) 
We expect September revenue will go up by 6% MoM to Rmb827mn/NT$3,930mn, 
mainly driven by the improvement in daily pneumatic revenue (Rmb30.9mn in Sep vs. 
Rmb 29.1mn in Aug) despite the same number of working days. The improvement in 
daily pneumatic revenue is driven by the strong growth from key customers, particularly 
in electronics (we also expect peers to see strong demand in electronics, see here), 
followed by batteries and machine tools, and we expect demand to improve following 
the inventory pull-in after the Europe summer holiday.

Investment view: Strong YoY momentum in electronics could drive overall growth. 
Maintain positive view on AirTAC 
We reiterate our Buy rating on AirTAC, as we continue to see key end-segments 
performance tracking ahead of the company’s 2026 full year guidance of 20%+ YoY

### Page 3

growth (see here), with electronics/battery/machine tools delivering 20%/64%/31% 
YTD YoY growth while total revenue in Rmb is tracking at 25% YTD YoY growth. The 
strong YTD performance is in line with our expectation that AirTAC is a key beneficiary 
of the factory automation/industrial automation localization trend in mainland China, as 
well as benefiting from the prolonged upcycle supported by government stimulus 
policies. However, we maintain caution on the potential of exaggerated orders (including 
advance or duplicate orders), and will continue to monitor the underlying end-demand 
closely. 
Furthermore, we believe the company is well-positioned to continue to gain market 
share in the China pneumatic market, particular given its pricing strategy, (1) its 
pneumatic products pricing remain competitively priced at 10%+ below international 
peers, and (2) the company has maintained stable pricing in China (China accounts for 
~95% of total revenue in 2026E per GSe) amid the inflationary cost environment, while 
the company will continue to enhance internal operational efficiency to mitigate the 
rising cost risk (see here). As a result, we continue to see backlog accumulating and 
expect the company to maintain a high UTR to digest these orders. 
Maintain Buy on AirTAC with an unchanged 12m TP of NT$1,925. 
Investment Thesis - AirTAC 
AirTAC is the 2nd-largest pneumatic supplier in mainland China with 28% market share 
in 2025, outpacing most local competitors, owing to its low-cost yet high quality 
product, and service quality reputation in most key cities in mainland China. We forecast 
its market share will reach 30% in 2026E and expect AirTAC to be the structural winner 
in a deflationary market, due to its: (1) strong market growth and leadership: AirTAC 
continues to expand its market share in mainland China driven by competitive pricing 
that is more than 10% lower vs. international peers (with 45%+ GM / 30-35% OPM) and 
high demand elasticity among mainland China customers; and (2) superior cost structure 
and financial health: with best-in-class cash cost and a solid balance sheet, featuring 
positive net cash over FCF since 2023/2024, we believe AirTAC is well-equipped to 
weather industry downturns and capitalize on market restructuring. 
Overall, we believe AirTAC will see better revenue/profitability in the long term given its 
share gainer position in the China pneumatic market and potential to gain share in the 
linear guide market (improved demand in the next 1-2 years). This should lead to 
strengthened valuation, vs. its recent trading range (trading below its 10-year avg. P/E 
multiple). We are Buy rated on AirTAC. 
Price target risks and methodology - AirTAC 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$1,925 is based on a target P/E of 26.0x on 
our 2027E EPS (0.25x STDV above avg of the last pneumatic upcycle). 
Key downside risks: (1) Stronger competition from peers; (2) sharper-than-expected 
Rmb depreciation, and (3) a longer-than-expected end-market demand downtrend in 
mainland China.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
On the latest earnings call, Broadcom's CEO noted that the company is building
substrate capacity in its Singapore factory with a partner. We believe he was
referring to AST, the JV Broadcom has been developing with Toppan (7911.T, Not
Covered) since March 2024 (news). This is Toppan's first overseas substrate plant,
with phase 1 of the two-phase fab targeted to start production by EoY 2026. We see

Broadcom securing FC-BGA substrate supply rather than bringing production in-
house, and therefore expect limited impact on the current supply positions of
Unimicron and NYPCB.
On BT substrate pricing, the price hikes since 2H25 have been driven by raw material
cost inflation, including T glass, gold and copper. We have expected T glass supply
constraints to peak in 2026 and gradually ease from 2027 as more suppliers are
qualified. Thus, it would not surprise us if raw-material-driven price hikes plateau

going forward. That said, our OW thesis on Unimicron and NYPCB centers on ABF
substrate tightness, so we see limited impact from BT price fluctuations on our
ratings for both stocks.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
NYPCB's share price was down 12% in the past two trading days (vs. TAIEX up
0.8%).
Investors have asked us if NYPCB would face share losses if Broadcom (AVGO,
covered by Joe Moore) were to produce substrates on its own.
Separately, we have been receiving questions from investors about the pricing
outlook for BT substrate in 4Q.

We see limited impact on our OW thesis for Unimicron and NYPCB, both of which
produce BT substrates and are Broadcom's incumbent suppliers.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 15%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers

n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Nan Ya PCB (8046.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate

value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.3%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 17%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF and BT demand
n Faster-than-expected pickup in AI and 5G demand
n Stronger-than-expected ASP hike
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.

CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall, creating headwinds for ABF substrate demand and pricing
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

### Page 1

ab 6 September 2026
Global Research
Largan Precision
Strong CPO ramp priced ahead of qualification
The re-rating has outpaced qualification progress; downgrade from Buy to Sell
Largan shares have risen 242% since April on optimism around its CPO FAU progress.
While we stay constructive on Largan's CPO prospects, we believe the market is pricing
in confidence ahead of still-early qualification progress, particularly given a crowded
competitive field. Largan only starts its sample line late Q3, integrating its Prism Micro

Lens Array and Fiber Array with AOET's V-groove and Corning's fiber. We expect  several
quarters to improve yields, gain integrator acceptance (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and
convert its initial production-oriented FA program into volume while competing against
incumbent TFC and vertically integrated rivals incl. Coherent. We downgrade our rating
from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-
18x range well above our sum-of-the-parts NT$5,000 PT based on 15x smartphone lens

+ 50x CPO base case for 40% share  (upper end of optical peers  at 14-72x).
Still a range of outcomes for CPO solution traction
Largan's approach pairs V-groove with PMLA to shape the optical light from the PIC into
the fiber at high accuracy with automated assembly. Feedback is positive on Largan's
precision and track record for volume production/yield improvement (now still low) and
1 FA design in an FAU. A risk is Largan targeting the Gen 3 optics should take until 2028

and also competes with incumbent TFC which is already designed-in for 2026-27 and
other FAU vendors like Coherent that can vertically integrate or use meta lens not
requiring Largan's initial V-groove approach. Largan's push toward a complete solution
may also conflict with integration partners.
Core smartphone lens tracking behind amid memory headwinds
We also see risk on near-term results with Q326 sales tracking light. Aug sales were NT
$5bn, +3% MoM, -16% YoY, taking QTD revenue to NT$10.1bn, 53% of UBSe/Street

Q326. Sept would need to grow +73% MoM to meet UBSe/Street +36-38% QoQ vs.
normal seasonal +8% MoM. We expect flattish builds at best for its flagship customer
(80% of sales) needing to raise price to pass on memory costs +US$200-300 YoY albeit
some support from high-end introducing variable-aperture lens, while foldable lens
specs remain similar. Factoring muted smartphone growth but healthy margins, we view
a mid-teens multiple appropriate for this business, in line with its historical range.

Stock prices in our upside case ahead of traction; risk-reward skews negative
We maintain basic '26E/27E EPS at NT$190/NT$214 and NT$5,000 SOTP PT based on 1)
15x smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730
(40% share, 100k switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of
peers due to its premium ~40-45% OpM. We view risk/reward skewed negative with
downside to NT$2,500 on limited CPO success and softer smartphones, base case NT

$5,000 for CPO traction, and upside 22% if the stock is bid to our upside case NT$9,000
based on top-end 70x P/E on CPO earnings at 60% share of a higher US$1.58bn TAM.

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 2
Largan Precision UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Could Largan's CPO products contribute meaningful revenue in a few years?
We stay constructive but the company still has hurdles to clear in a crowded space. Its scale and track
record in high-end smartphone lens gives it an opportunity supplying key components of the CPO

FAU assembly, traditional strength in precision lens micro assemblies, alignment accuracy, in-house
tooling and automation that could give it a differentiated solution. Our feedback at Semicon Taiwan
suggests the company is still early in qualification, with its sample production line for customer
evaluation readied in late September and still requiring work with a system integrator and
qualification with the fabless customer and ODMs.  We still see a year to move from sampling to

design-in but if successful timed to meet expected market acceleration in 2028-29.
Q: Will there be further technology upgrades driving the lens cycle in the coming years?
We expect spec upgrades for Largan's core lens business to come in H226 from variable aperture
shipments for the iPhone 18 (which Genius does not currently participate though may enter over
time, as seen with periscope lenses). Key offset though is weaker sales with Q3 tracking light and

suggesting a softer build due to memory headwinds. Further upside could come from broader
adoption of 48MP modules in future iPhone models. Looking longer term, Apple is reportedly
exploring 200MP periscope lens and adjacent opportunities to penetrate physical AI categories with
precision lens including robotics, autonomous systems and AR/VR devices.
We downgrade our rating on Largan from Buy to Sell as we view the market expectations as now
pricing in a bullish scenario for strong market traction with the company still at an early stage of

qualification, still needing to improve its process and yields, and competing in a crowded space also
against an existing incumbent. We expect several quarters to improve current low yields, gain
acceptance from solution integrators (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and secure design-ins by the AI GPU
fabless company and its ODM partners against incumbent TFC and other rivals. We downgrade
Largan from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-18x

range and well above our sum-of-the-parts NT$5,000 price target based on 15x smartphone lens +
50x CPO base case for40% share  (upper-end of optical peer range of 14-72x).
Largan has demonstrated its samples with partners at Computex and Semicon Taiwan and cited its
goals to ramp CPO as its next opportunity. The company is still early in qualifications with a sample
automated line in the coming months followed by design-in engagement with system integrators,

ODMs and the fabless customer to target design-in on 3rd gen CPO solutions in 2028. For
smartphone lens, its top customer had a strong iPhone 17 cycle although acknowledged it would
have to raise price to pass on higher memory costs which could dampen growth. Sales QTD are
tracking slightly light as Largan also competes with 2nd source Genius. Our supply chain checks and
management commentary points to introduction of higher value variable aperture lens in H226.

Largan is currently trading above the high end of its historical range at 39x/35x our 2026E/27E EPS vs.
a long-term range of 13-18x forward consensus earning, pricing in success penetrating the CPO
component market ahead of the earnings stream which would only start late in 2027 into 2028. We
note optical component peers trade at 14-72x with an average of ~38x 2027E P/E (Genius at 25x
2027 P/E). We maintain our SOTP valuation with our price target at NT$5000 based on 1) 15x

smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730 (40% share, 100k
switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of peers due to its premium ~40-
45% OpM. We view the stock at NT$7,400 now pricing in CPO traction on a 50x multiple of our
upside NT$76 case based on 60% share of an upside US$1.6bn opportunity.

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 3
Largan Precision UBS Research
commentary
Largan shares have risen 242% since 1 April on optimism around its CPO fiber array unit
(FAU) progress. While we stay constructive post our feedback at Semicon Taiwan on
Largan's ability to develop CPO solutions, we now believe the market is pricing in a level
of confidence that has run well ahead of qualification progress which is still early and
competing in a crowded field. Largan will only start its sample line in late Q3 integrating

its Prism Micro Lens Array and Fiber Array, AOET's V-groove and Corning's glass fiber.
From there, we expect several quarters to improve current low yields, gain acceptance
from solution integrators (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and secure design-ins by the AI
GPU fabless company and its ODM partners against incumbent TFC and rivals including
Coherent with own lens/FAU. The company did note on its Q226 call that it had secured
one mass production commitment for its fiber array to an FAU maker that could ramp by

H227 although early to conclude the magnitude of this engagement or end customer
design-in, with the other customers still at proof-of-concept phase.
We downgrade our rating on Largan from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and
the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-18x range and well above our sum-of-the-parts NT
$5,000 price target based on 15x smartphone lens + 50x CPO base case for 40% share
(upper-end of optical component peers trading at a  range of 14-72x).

Still a range of outcomes for CPO solution traction
Largan's approach pairs V-groove with PMLA to shape the optical light from the PIC into
the fiber at high accuracy with automated assembly. Feedback is positive on Largan's
precision and track record for volume production/yield improvement (now still low). A
risk is Largan targeting the Gen 3 optics should take until 2028 and also competes with

incumbent TFC which is already designed-in for 2026-27 and other FAU vendors like
Coherent that can vertically integrate or use meta lens not requiring Largan's initial V-
groove approach. Largan's business model may also conflict with integration partners as
it also attempts to build toward a complete solution.
We estimate Largan's Total Addressable Market in a downside case is US$270mn if it
only supplies sub-components into a market that continues to push out CPO due to

reliability concerns relative to pluggables and NPO/XPO options, US$1.08bn in a base
case with 100k switches and 72 FAUs per switch at US$150 ASP , and US$1.6bn in an
upside case with 125k switches, 72 FAUs per switch and US$175 ASP .  For Largan, we
estimate downside case 10% share yields NT$810mn sales and NT$2.20 EPS, base case
40% share, NT$13.0bn sales and NT$35 EPS, and upside case 60% share, NT$28.4bn
sales and NT$76 EPS.
Although we are optimistic on the market opportunity scaling up and Largan's

competencies and progress developing these solutions, Largan's traction is not yet
certain and it will compete in a crowded landscape. We believe Himax is already
supplying wafer level optics for the collimator function with FOCI (3363.TWO) and TFC
(300394.SZ) already has demonstrated capability and design-in on the FAU and lens
assembly. Others that could vertically integrate include Coherent, Senko, Corning and
Sumitomo, and we also note Genius sending samples and Sunny Optical's intention to

develop for this market.

Our feedback at Semicon Taiwan indicates Coherent can make the FAU and lens by itself
through capabilities merging with II-VI and demonstrated all in-house components at
the booth except for the metal transceiver casing.

Smartphone lens business tracking behind, should have a mild 2026-27
Largan reported August sales at NT$5bn,   is below seasonal 63-67% at only 53% of
Q326 UBSe/Street +36-38% QoQ and down 16% YoY, suggesting muted momentum
into its flagship customer (~80% of sales). July EPS was NT$13.94, 27% of UBSe Q3 NT
$51.52 suggesting in-line GM/OpM at ~50%/39% but the lighter sales are more of a

concern.
UBS expects flattish builds at best and a mild spec upgrade with one model variable
aperture but foldable at similar lens spec and higher memory cost (+US$200-300 YoY).
We still see mild growth and mid-teens multiple acceptable for the smartphone
business, similar to its long-term average.

Stock prices in our  upside case ahead of traction; risk-reward skews
negative
We maintain basic '26E/27E EPS at NT$190/NT$214 and NT$5,000 SOTP PT based on 1)
15x smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730
(40% share, 100k switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of
peers due to its premium ~40-45% OpM. We view risk/reward skewed negative with
downside to NT$2,500 on limited CPO success and softer smartphones, base case NT

$5,000 for CPO traction and upside 22% if the stock is bid to our upside case NT$9,000
based on top-end 70x P/E on CPO earnings based on 60% share/125k switches/9mn
FAUs, US$175 FAU ASP , US$1.58bn TAM).

Largn (3008.TW) trading at 7,400 at closed Sept 4.

UPSIDE (NT$9,000): Our upside valuation of NT$3,655 is based on  its average P/E of 16x
off our 2027 upside case EPS of NT$235. We factor in faster than expected
CPO progress to deliver upside EPS of NT$76 and 70x P/E at the high end of optical peers
trading at 14-72x 2027E P/E to an implied valuation of NT$5,345, and  total share price at
NT$9,000.
BASE (NT$5,000):  Our price target for its smartphone business is NT$3,270, with a 15x
2027 P/E applied on its base case EPS of NT$214 slightly below the long-term average of

its historical P/E band to reflect the macro uncertainty. Paired with our base scenario of its
CPO business at NT$35 EPS and 50x P/E, we arrive at an implied valuation of NT$1,730,
and Largan's aggregate PT  at NT$5,000.
DOWNSIDE (NT$2,500):   Downside valuation at NT$2,392, based on low-end of its
range 13x 2027 P/E. We take into consideration slower adoption of FAUs at its downside
EPS of NT$2 and the same 50x P/E to  an implied valuation of NT$108 for

CPO contribution, and Largan's total share price at NT$2,500.

Forecast returns
Forecast price appreciation -32.4%
Forecast dividend yield 1.1%
Forecast stock return -31.4%
Market return assumption 6.3%
Forecast excess return -37.6%
Company Description
Founded in 1987, Largan Precision (Largan) is one of the world's leading manufacturers of
lenses used in cameras and IT products. It specialises in plastic/hybrid lenses and, to a lesser
extent, glass lenses and modules. In 2022, 20MP+ camera lenses were 10-20% of Largan's

shipment mix, 10MP lenses were 50-60%, 8MP lenses were 0-10% and other lenses were
30-40%.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on SOTP valuation on the CPO opportunity and traditional lens
business.
We think Largan's downside risks include: 1) ongoing pricing pressure from smartphone OEM
clients and increasingly intense competition among lens suppliers; and 2) a slower-than-
expected smartphone demand recovery in China, and 3) CPO commercial success below the

market's elevated expectations.
We believe the key upside risk is a faster-than-expected CPO qualification process at Largan.
In addition, if the company demonstrates exceptionally efficient and highly accurate
automation capabilities, it could support greater market share gains within the industry than
we currently anticipate.

horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

ramp-up of D2C satellites from 2H27, with PCB area consumption to further increase by
40-50%, and (3) the ramp-up of AI satellites from 2H27, with PCB dollar content value
that is similar to LEO satellites, but at a significantly larger annual deployment volume of
several tens of thousands of units (vs. 3-4K LEO satellites launched by the key LEO
customer in 2025).
(6) The company mentioned that it is seeing some impact from material shortages, but

did mention the supply conditions for low-end CCL are improving, as supply has been
primarily constrained by E-glass. However, management mentioned there are no supply
issues for high-end CCL, while supply is well-supported by key CCL suppliers. In terms of
rising material costs, the company said it can pass through cost increase across nearly all
products, except for SLP products for the key smartphone customer.
(7) The company said it is seeing multiple growth opportunities for its data center PCB

business, including (1) strong demand for high-end PCB for AI satellites from its key LEO
customers (as mentioned in bullet point 4), (2) PCB for AI servers from the key
smartphone customer, which is expected to ramp-up from 4Q26, (3) additional
contributions from China customers with PCB demand across switches and AI servers,
and (4) an ongoing project with a small-scale ASIC client.
PCB/CCL sector read-across
We retain our positive view on the optical transceivers’ mSAP demand outlook, while our

supply chain checks recently suggested mSAP as a key bottleneck for the 1.6T optical
transceivers supply chain now, whose product pricing has gone up by 30%+ YTD with
good profitability (our checks and Compeq’s comments suggest the GM for 1.6T optical
transceivers mSAP are generally 40-45%+). We believe ZDT has the best opportunity to
gain more market share in this high-growth and profitable market (we expect TAM for
1.6T+ optical transceivers mSAP to go up from US$0.8bn in 2026 to US$2.9bn in 2028,

implying 94% CAGR), considering it has the most aggressive mSAP capacity expansion
plan (we expect ZDT to expand mSAP capacity by 5x in the next two years) and the
company’s best-in-class BT/mSAP production technology, which could become the
company’s key revenue driver (we expect mSAP to account for ~8%/10%+ of ZDT
revenue in 2027/28 from ~2% in 2026) and margin driver (mSAP GM should be 40-45%+
vs. ZDT 1H26 GM at only ~22+%).
We also expect CCL suppliers, such as EMC and TUC, to benefit from the strong optical

transceiver demand, and we expect both companies to gain more share with more CCL
capacity to be ready in 2027/28 (see here and here).
Overall, we retain our positive view on ZDT’s business outlook not only on mSAP
products, but also seeing a significant margin expansion opportunity from the high-end
AI PCB and ABF substrate products, where we also see a strong demand outlook (see our
AI PCB industry report here and our ABF substrate industry report here).

We maintain our Buy ratings on ZDT, EMC and TUC with unchanged 12m TPs of
NT$925/NT$10,200/NT$3,010.

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - Zhen Ding Technology 
Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT) is the global leading PCB supplier (top 1 in market share in 
sales) with 7-10% market share, and started its ABF substrate business from 2023 in 
Shenzhen, China; it has the highest ABF revenue exposure to the China market compared 
to all other ABF substrate suppliers. We believe ZDT will hold more than 40% ABF 
substrate market share in China by 2027E (up from 31% in 2024) and benefit the most 
from the strong and growing China ABF substrate market (53% 2025E-27E CAGR) due to 
the increasing in-sourcing ratio for the AI computing ASIC/GPU market in China. We also 
expect the increasing foldable smartphone penetration rate in the long term to drive 
ZDT’s FPC TAM, and account for 50% of the company’s total revenue in 2027E. We see 
further revenue upside potential from foldable phones. We are Buy rated mainly due to 
our positive view on the company’s China ABF substrate business opportunity, which 
should not only drive ZDT’s revenue growth, but also improve its OPM level in the long 
term. We see upside for the stock price considering the company’s solid growth 
opportunity, and our TP implied P/E multiple is still lower than the company’s past 10+ 
year P/E average, which we believe is fair considering the potential upside from China 
ABF substrate market and increasing foldable phone penetration rate.  
Valuation: Our 12-month TP of NT$925 is based on a SOTP valuation methodology, 
including (1) 3.5x blended 2028E P/B for ABF & BT substrate, (2) 20x 2028E P/E for 
high-growth AI server/switch related PCB (in line with industry average), and (3) 8x 
2028E P/E for low-growth consumer electronics PCB (in-line with industry downcycle 
average valuation).  
Key downside risks include: (1) weaker-than-expected iPhone demand or slower 
content upgrade, (2) delay in new substrate capacity or slower-than-expected yield rate 
ramp up progress, and (3) fiercer competition in China ABF market. 
Investment Thesis - Elite Material  
EMC, a key supplier of high-end HDI material (70%+ market share) and SLP material 
(90%+ market share), has been focusing on the high-speed switch/server CCL market for 
3-5+ years. We are positive on its market share trajectory in the server industry and 
expect EMC to increase its share from <10% in the Purley generation to 15-20%/20+% 
in the Whitley/Eagle Stream platforms. EMC is also proactively expanding its share in the 
switch market (40%/30%+ in 2024/23 vs. <5% before 2019), with an expanding AI 
customer base (only one 400G customer in 3Q20, to being one of the largest suppliers in 
2023), which should continue to benefit the company’s top/bottom line growth over 
time. We believe EMC’s leading position in the AI server CCL market should drive strong 
revenue growth and GM/OPM expansion in the long term, given that it is the major 
supplier for all Nvidia/Google/AWS AI server projects, and we expect it to maintain its 
leadership position. Considering its leading position in the high-end CCL industry, solid 
production skills and earnings growth outlook, we are Buy rated on the stock. With the 
shares trading at a 2026E P/E below AI server component suppliers and supported by a 
solid growth outlook, we view valuation as attractive. 
Key downside risks include: (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) 
rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Elite Material  
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$10,200 is based on 27x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(in line with the peak P/E multiple for AI component suppliers in the past 3 years).  
Key downside risks:  (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) rising

rising competition from mainland China peers. 
Investment Thesis - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
TUC is a key high-end global CCL supplier that focuses on M7+ grade high-speed CCL 
(key applications include high-end switches (100G+) and AI server materials), with 20%+ 
market share in the past two years. The company continues to expect its unit market 
share in the server industry to increase from 15% in Whitley and Purley processors to 
higher in the Eagle Stream generation, and plans to launch two types of products for 
low-end and high-end customers (T2A and T2C) to gain market share. Also, strong 
growth in 400G/800G switch shipments and new 800G switch shipments, scheduled for 
launch in 2025-27, should continue to be a key demand driver for TUC, in our view. 
Additionally, the company is working on high-end AI projects with CSP and enterprise 
players, which we believe should drive revenue momentum in the long term. We view the 
stock as undervalued vs. Taiwan CCL peers on a P/E basis and rate it Buy.  
Key downside risks include: (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL 
segment; (2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch 
shipments globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$3,010 is based on a 22x 2027E P/E (+2x 
STDV higher than the past 3-year industry average P/E multiple).  
Key downside risks:  (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL segment; 
(2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch shipments 
globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that

積極轉向 HDD、半導體、軍工等高毛利率業務 
 給予買進，目標價 80 元：投顧認為正達具備半導體應用、無人機與
AI 等題材。且 HDD 業務 2027 年第四季月產能達 500 萬片，未來年
產值可達約 60 億元，營運可望出現爆發成長，給予買進，目標價 80
元。 
 預期 3Q26 毛利率優於前期，加以認列廠房處分利益，EPS 2.07 元：
展望 26H2，正達表示營運高峰有望在 3Q26，且閒置的銅鑼三廠出售
預期在 3Q26 完成交割。整體而言，投顧估計正達 3Q26 營收 6.28 億
元，+13.2%QoQ，+6.1%YoY，毛利率回升，加以認列銅鑼三廠處分
利益，EPS 2.07 元。 
 HDD 需求夯，正達積極拚擴產：正達近期積極切入 AI、無人機、半導
體等高毛利率領域。正達以超低平坦度拋光、奈米級剪薄、3D 曲面鍍
膜等技術切入 HDD 供應鏈，目前已建置月產能 10 萬片，預計 2026
年底擴至月產能 100 萬片，2027 年第四季達月產能 500 萬片，2028
年過後若滿載年產值約達 60 億元， 以淨利率15%為假設 ， 可貢獻EPS 
3~4 元。至於軍工與無人機領域與美國國防系統整合商、北約系統商
簽訂多項框架協議，合作航太軍工級玻璃適形天線與軍用無人機，若
順利 2027 年第 2 季可望開始出貨。

正達 產品為 玻 璃 的 精 密 加
工，主 要 業 務 為 光電 、車 用 、
建築 等應用  
 正達(3149) 成立於 1996 年 6 月，2011 年 11 月掛牌上市 ，為台灣最大的光電玻
璃加工服務供應商，擁有玻璃切割、強化、薄化、鍍膜等完整加工服務。總部位
於台灣苗栗，生產基地位於台灣苗栗、中國觀瀾及成都、越南。主要產品包括：
保護玻璃、光學鍍膜玻璃、ITO 鍍膜玻璃，產品應用於蘋果電腦及平板、汽車、
建築。主要客戶為蘋果、藍思、群創、GIS-KY。正達與美商康寧簽署車載玻璃
戰略合作備忘錄，在汽車座艙與顯示應用上，結合上游玻璃材料與下游加工能
力，有利守住車用高階市場，並同步受惠國際品牌導入新設計 。

元 
 正達 2Q26 營收 5.54 億元，-6.1%QoQ，-2.1%YoY，營收下滑主要是因蘋果議
價導致，毛利率從前其-4.1%升至-3.2%，營業費用為 0.89 億元，營業損失約為
1.07 億元，稅後淨損 1.08 億元，EPS -0.48 元。展望 26H2，正達表示營運高
峰有望在 3Q26，且閒置的銅鑼三廠出售預期在 3Q26 完成交割。整體而言，投
顧估計正達 3Q26 營收 6.28 億元，+13.2%QoQ，+6.1%YoY，毛利率回升，加
以認列銅鑼三廠處分利益，EPS 2.07 元。

正達表示 2026 年成長動能主要來自智能汽車 產品，智能建築及智能光電將下
滑；預期今年底智能汽車營收占比將從目前的 14%升至 20%。整體而言，正達
預期 2026 年營收可維持小幅成長局面，且毛利率受到產品組合優化，預計將持
續上升，投顧預期正達 2026 年營收 23.83 億元，年增 4.4%。

HDD 需 求 夯，正 達 積 極 拚 擴
產  
 正達近期積極切入 AI、無人機、半導體等高毛利率領域。隨 AI 伺服器帶動 3.5 
吋 HDD 需求，HAMR 對高溫玻璃基板的需求，未來大容量 HDD 以玻璃碟盤
為主。正達以超低平坦 度拋光、奈米級剪薄、 3D 曲面鍍膜等技術切入， 目前
已建置月產能 10 萬片，預計 2026 年底擴至月產能 100 萬片，2027 年第四季
達月產能 500 萬片，2028 年過後若滿載年產值約達 60 億元，以淨利率 15%為
假設，可貢獻 EPS 3~4 元。

倚強科 (3219 TT)  Buy

受惠伺服器 \消費性電子產品測試需求， 2Q26 獲利大幅成
長，給 Buy 的投資建議。  
投資建議 ：基於考量到倚強科受惠客戶分散供應鏈， AI 伺服器
\AR 眼鏡帶動測試設備需求，毫米波解決方案具發展潛力，給
Buy 的投資建議。  
公司簡介： 倚強科目前主要產品包含電路板測試解決方案、毫
米波 (5G) 陣列天線。測試設備的應用又可以分為消費性電子、車
用、伺服器。  
倚強科的產品主要用於電子產品的 PCBA 的相關測試： 倚強科
的設備可應用於消費電子，包含手機、手錶、耳機、平板和穿戴
式裝置 … 等主流消費性電子產品，也可應用在自駕車、伺服器、
多項工業 … 等領域。  
倚強科有受惠於客戶分散供應鏈： 目前客戶有將自身供應鏈從
中國轉出，未來會有越來越多的產品線移到印度， 2026 年倚強
科有拿到耳機相關的測試設備的訂單，倚強科的中國對手為上
市公司，做的產品線比較廣。  
倚強科有提供毫米波解決方案，主要瞄準印度市場： 倚強科做
毫米波陣列天線模組 (Antenna-in-module ，AiM) ，合作廠商包含
台灣 5G SoC 廠商義傳科技。 產品應用包含 5G 專網、 FWA ，
ODM 可直接採用倚強科 AiM 模組給客戶做 POC( 概念驗證 )，縮
短毫米波模組開發時程 ， 目前客戶包含印度 \美國 \日本電信運營
商、系統整合商。  
群益預估倚強科 2026 年 /2027 年稅後 EPS 5.10 元 /7.99 元： 群
益預估倚強科 2026 年 /2027 年營收為 23.32 億元 /28.00 億元，
YoY+36.91%/+20.08% ；稅後純益 3.66 億元 /5.74 億元，
YoY+237.44%/+56.83% ；稅後 EPS 5.10 元 /7.99 元。

3 群益投顧 
倚強科的產品主要用於電子產品的 PCBA 的相關測試：  
倚強科的設備可應用於消費電子，包含手機、手錶、耳機、平板和穿戴式裝
置 … 等主流消費性電子產品，也可應用在自駕車、伺服器、多項工業 … 等領
域。  
倚強科自主研發的設備為 T1000 ，模組化設計有助於客戶客製化需求、快速
插拔的介面可配合客戶產品線的切換，此設備是用於伺服器的測試，從軟硬
體到現場支援都是由倚強科所服務。  
T1000 是一款在板級測試中不可或缺的工具，提供了多種功能性。這款全面
的測試設備整合了倚強科技的插件模塊、堅固的外型設計和專有的測試軟
件，針對各種測試需求進行了定制。支援多達 1024 個引腳， T1000 可容納
各種電路板尺寸，並應用於物聯網、 5G、汽車和能源等領域等。  
倚強科還有推出 Board Function Test(BTF) ，主要模擬伺服器主機板使用的
情形，用於判斷主機板是否能用於最後的組裝。  
倚強科在 AR 眼鏡的客戶包含美系品牌廠，此客戶預計 2027 年會發表雙目
眼鏡，另一美系客戶於 2026 年會發表第一代產品。美系客戶的第一代客戶
的 AR 眼鏡重量較重，希望下一代的產品可以做到長時間配戴，需要做機構
上的測試。  
伺服器部分，倚強科有出貨設備到馬來西亞、墨西哥、越南、泰國和中國，
並持續開拓新市場，倚強科客戶主要為台系 ODM ，倚強科伺服器的測試設
備的主要對手產品為 Keysight 3070 ，市佔率達 97% ，倚強科的產品價格只
有對手的 1/10 ，倚強科目前還有在接觸美系 CPU/GPU 大廠，會把相關設備
做更多的客製化，現在在送樣客戶的北美實驗。  
目前伺服器的主機板的測試設備用的還是 Keysight 的解決方案，其他 PCB
的測試設備之後有望轉用倚強科的解決方案，未來伺服器的測試難度變高，
相關 PCB 板尺寸與重量會大幅增加，為了減少測試所需要的空間，測試設
備會變成集成式的方式，來增加整體的產能。  
由於倚強科有出色的研發與全球服務能力，經過兩年與客戶深入合作與技
術考驗，客戶已於 2026 年正式通知倚強科參與 TPU 的專案。  
客戶主要在台系 OSAT 做封測，未來量產將由台系 OSAT 來下單，為了因
應客戶需求，倚強科未來可能會去東南亞設新產能。  
2Q26 獲利大幅季增：  
倚強科 2Q26 營收 7.04 億元， QoQ+63.05%/YoY+31.10% ；稅後純益 1.57 億
元， QoQ+360.78%/YoY+146.53% ；稅後 EPS 2.18 元。  
2Q26 毛利率達 72% ，季增約 8.5ppt ，原因有三點。一、在印度的投資效益
顯現，品質 \交期 \服務都優於同業。二、應用於 AI 伺服器的營收占比有提
升。三、加上 AR 眼鏡的投入有挹注營收。  
倚強科有受惠於客戶分散供應鏈：  
倚強科可以提供軟體、硬體、機構件、電子…等，目前主要競爭對手是中國

### Page 4

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 8 月 28 日

4 群益投顧 
廠商。倚強科在台灣、中國、印度都有生產基地，現在美系客戶在拉高非中
國的供應鏈，對倚強科有利。  
目前客戶有將自身供應鏈從中國轉出，未來會有越來越多的產品線移到印
度， 2026 年倚強科有拿到耳機相關的測試設備的訂單，倚強科的中國對手
為上市公司，產品線比較廣。  
因應地緣政治，大部分伺服器的 ODM 會去找台灣的供應鏈，對於倚強科有
利；中國商機部分，倚強科可以用子公司去接單，目前中國營收已經從之前
的超過 80% ，降低至約 35% 。  
倚強科有提供毫米波解決方案，主要瞄準印度市場：  
5G 可分為 FR1( 包含 Sub-6GHz 以下的頻段 )、 FR2(mmWave)(24~71GHz) ，
倚強科只做 FR2 ，應用包含戶外的 FWA 和室內的企業專網，倚強科主要是
瞄準印度市場。  
倚強科做毫米波陣列天線模組 (Antenna-in-module ， AiM) ，合作廠商包含台
灣 5G SoC 廠商義傳科技。產品應用包含 5G 專網、 FWA，ODM 可直接採用
倚強科 AiM 模組給客戶做 POC( 概念驗證 )，縮短毫米波模組開發時程，目
前客戶包含印度 \美國 \日本電信運營商、系統整合商。  
毫米波一開始原本要應用在行動通訊，現在縮減到特定的領域，室外包含
FWA( 點對點、點對多點 )，室內像是企業專網、私人網路、工廠。  
FWA 可應用於 Telecom( 電信 )\私人網路。 FR2 未來發展的情況要看政府的
標案，中國目前只有給企業專網。印度現在積極推動寬頻基建，倚強科投入
的點對點、 5G 毫米波的網路架構，可以用來解決光纖網路布建不足的問題。
倚強科已在印度成立子公司，有和系統廠商合作，正在開發 Wi-Fi over 
mmWave 的產品，現在已經進入了實地測試的階段，倚強科提前卡位印度電
子製造市場。  
倚強科的天線裡面的晶片可以做更換，中國廠商可以用中國製的晶片，天線
模組裡面沒有記憶體，不受記憶體漲價的影響。

圖片來源：倚強科技。

5 群益投顧 
倚強科對於 2027 年營運表示樂觀：  
倚強科樂觀看待未來營運， 2027 年營運成長動能包含美系品牌客戶持續擴
充非中國地區的產能、兩間美系品牌眼鏡客戶的測試需求、 AI 伺服器持續
帶動 PCBA 相關設備需求。  
其他補充：  
倚強科已經和美系客戶合作多年， 2025 年自駕車的產品的相關營收占比約
5%，估 2026 年占比翻倍成長。  
接單到出貨的時間要看客戶的排程，以美系大客戶為例，完整的交期約 12
個月。伺服器的測試設備約 6~8 周，之後在 6 周以內，伺服器用的治具的交
期約 4~6 周。  
倚強科之前傳統旺季是第二季，因為現在客戶策略變更，將手機的旗艦機種
與一般機種分開發表，現在對倚強科而言 Q2~Q4 都是旺季。  
由於消費性電子的測試需求變複雜，伺服器相關測試需求暢旺，倚強科希望
合併後平均毛利率可達 60% 。

### Page 1

J.P. Morgan Securities (Asia Pacific) Limited/ J.P.
Morgan Broking (Hong Kong) Limited
Credo (CRDO US, OW, covered by Joseph Cardoso), which is BizLink’s active
electrical cable (AEC) contract manufacturing customer , reported its FY1Q27
results. Key takeaways include:
• FY1Q27 results and FY2Q27 guidance. Credo reported FY1Q27 revenue of
US$479mn (+115% YoY, +10% QoQ) and delivered a 68% non-GAAP gross
margin. Non-GAAP net income rose to US$236.3mn (+140% YoY, +4%

QoQ), coming in above consensus. The top four customers each contributed
≥10% of revenue, with the largest at 33%, followed by 28%, 13% and 10%.
Looking into FY2Q27, Credo guided to revenue of US$525-535mn (midpoint
+11% QoQ), with non-GAAP gross margin of 67-69%. Full-year FY27
revenue is expected to grow over 85% YoY, driven by more than US$600mn
in optical revenue, according to the company.
• AEC demand remains healthy with 1.6T extending the growth runway.

Credo’s AEC business continued to grow, with management highlighting
ongoing penetration gains at hyperscalers and neocloud customers.
Management also noted 1.6T AEC to begin contributing in FY2H27 and ramp
up more meaningfully in FY28, with its solutions extending up to 6.5 meters.
Under the strong growth ahead for the optical transceiver pluggable market
(i.e. 60mn units in 2026 to 175mn by 2030), the company expects to capture
the broader market expansion across not only the optical portfolio but also

AECs and other copper solutions.
• Optical is ramping rapidly, while ALC broadens growth opportunities.
Credo sees decent growth in its optical business, with management reiterating
optical DSPs, silicon photonics PICs and ZeroFlap optics to each contribute
over US$100mn in FY27 revenue. In addition, record retimer revenue and
increasing engagement in ALC and OmniConnect further reinforce its
comprehensive connectivity solutions. The company plans to demonstrate the

ALC solution at the Open Compute Project (OCP) Global Summit in October,
and it also remains on-target for an initial revenue in FY28.
• Implications for BizLink. Overall, the read-through from Credo’s
commentary remains positive for BizLink. While Credo’s AEC growth is
likely to lag optical in the near term, reflecting the faster ramp of optical from
a smaller base, management still expects copper and optical solutions to
maintain a more balanced mix over the longer term. This suggests that

Investment Thesis, Valuation and Risks
BizLink (Overweight; Price Target: NT$3,100.00)
Investment Thesis
We rate BizLink OW considering: 1) rising power demand and content value as AI platforms
transition to VR200 and VR Ultra; 2) additional growth opportunities from the ramp-up of
optical interconnect solutions and solid capital equipment business; and 3) strengthened

competitive positioning through close design-in collaboration with NVIDIA and
hyperscalers.
Valuation
Our Jun-27 PT of NT$3,100 is based on 23x of 12M forward EPS. The 23x valuation
multiple is at the higher end of the 10-year trading range, on the back of a solid growth
outlook thanks to rising exposure to high growth and margin-lucrative HPC and capital
equipment business.
Risks to Rating and Price Target
Key downside risks to our rating and price target include: 1) market-share loss amid

intensifying competition; 2) moderating AEC growth at higher data transmission speeds; 3)
gross margin pressure during platform and product transitions; and 4) weaker-than-
expected AI server demand and order growth.

大銀微系統 (4576 TT)  Buy

先進封裝、矽光子相關商機 2H26 持續擴大，評等為 Buy。 
投資建議 ：受惠於半導體前段製程、 CoWoS 測試設備相關訂單
挹注，以及矽光子檢測設備相關訂單數量自 07/2026 開始放大，
微米及奈米級定位系統 2H26 營收成長性可期，加上精密運動
及控制元件需求亦持續回溫，故群益的投資建議為 Buy 。  
2Q26 營業利益 QoQ+28.89% ，但受到匯兌損失影響，稅後淨利
QoQ-0.73% ：2Q26 營收 10.23 億元， QoQ+19.70% ，除精密運動
及控制元件營收動能續強外，微米及奈米級定位系統需求亦再
增溫，相關營收 5.34 億元， QoQ+17.20% ，雖然毛利率受到匯率
影響而降低至 37.10% ，但營業利益 QoQ+28.89% ，惟業外表現
不及 1Q26(1Q26 認列 0.10 億元匯兌利益， 2Q26 認列 0.37 億元
匯兌損失 )，稅後淨利 1.15 億元， QoQ-0.73% 。  
2H26 營收展望佳： 展望 2H26 ，微米及奈米級定位系統營收成
長性仍可期，目前訂單能見度達 5~6 個月，動能主要來自半導
體相關產品，原因有二， (1) 由於前段製程設備大廠訂單穩定挹
注，此部份商機主要來自台灣晶圓代工廠持續擴充先進製程，
(2) 台灣設備供應商 CoWoS 測試設備相關訂單數量持續增加，
且 CoPoS 、FOPLP 測試設備相關產品已送樣至客戶認證。此外，
矽光子檢測設備相關訂單自 04/2026 開始出貨給台灣半導體設
備供應商，且數量自 07/2026 開始放大， 07~08/2026 達數百台，
客戶並於 08/2026 兩度追加訂單，訂單能見度達 3~4 季。  
為因應微米及奈米級定位系統需求增溫，大銀微陸續啟動兩階
段擴產作業，第 1 階段已於 06/2026 完成，產能擴大約 20% ，
第 2 階段則預計於 2026 年年底至 2027 年年初之間完成，屆時
產能將再擴大 20% ，均為營收持續成長的後盾。  
至於精密運動及控制元件 2H26 營收展望亦佳，包括中國、東南
亞 (新加坡、印度等 )等地 PCB 鑽孔機、成型機設備供應商相關
需求自 4Q25 開始回溫，目前訂單能見度為 2.5~3 個月，營收成
長性較佳的產品包括線性馬達、伺服馬達、驅動器等。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 3 日

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就因應氣候變遷或溫室氣體管理之策略而言，大銀微成立 「氣候變遷風險小
組」，由董事長主持，主導全公司減碳因應方針及短、中、長期減碳策略，
依循氣候相關財務揭露建議書 (TCFD) 架構以進行氣候風險及機會評估，並
透過暴露程度與脆弱度矩陣研擬調適行動計畫，分別進行氣候變遷風險分
析評估潛在財務衝擊。  
再就企業溫室氣體排放量減量之預算與計畫而言，大銀微委託外部輔導團
隊協助教導 ISO 14064 溫室氣體盤查類別 3~類別 5 的執行項目，藉由盤查
間接溫室氣體所產生的碳排放數據，重整與規劃制定相關管理措施，使其降
低溫室氣體排放。至於大銀微持續進行節能減碳改善規劃方案則主要有五，
(1) 安裝節能智慧電表，結合節能 e 化系統管控電力使用，找出節能方案，
(2) 空壓機系統更換變頻空壓機或租賃馬力數較低的空氣用量， (3) 汰換冰水
機，選擇高效能的變頻冰水節能設備， (4) 分析找尋替代低碳及可回收材料，
並應用於產品循環經濟設計上， (5) 培養員工節能減碳的理念並養成節約用
電的好習慣。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

股價相對大盤走勢

環宇 -KY(4991 TT)  Buy

考量缺料問題逐漸改善、 2H26 營收展望佳，評等為 Buy。 
投資建議 ：雖然磷化銦基板供貨問題衝擊 2Q26 獲利表現，且
07/2026 營收仍受影響，不過，缺料狀況已逐漸改善，預估 KGD
將重拾成長動能， 2H26 業績可期，故群益對環宇 -KY 的投資建
議為 Buy 。  
2Q26 稅後淨利 1.10 億元， QoQ-43.82% ，惟仍為近幾年單季相
對高水準： 2Q26 營收 7.70 億元， QoQ+7.75% ，動能主要來自
RF & PE foundry ，但因產品組合不及 1Q26 ，毛利率降低至
43.27% ，營業利益 QoQ-46.17% ，稅後淨利減少至 1.10 億元，
QoQ-43.82% ，惟仍為近幾年單季相對高水準。  
若就 2Q26 產品結構而言，首先，季營收成長幅度最大者為 RF 
& PE foundry ，營收比重提高至 23%(1Q26 為 12%) ，原因在於
部份客戶考量通訊市場需求回溫而增加投產，相關營收增加至
1.77 億元， QoQ+106.53% ；其次， KGD 營收 4.70 億元， QoQ-
11.17% ，營收比重降低至 61%(1Q26 為 74%) ，主要係受到磷化
銦基板缺料影響；至於 OPTO foundry 營收 0.77 億元，
QoQ+7.75% ，營收比重 10% ，與 1Q26 相當。  
PD 為 2026 年主要營收動能； CW laser 業績想像空間大： 2026
年、 2027 年環宇 -KY 營收動能主要來自 KGD ，環宇 -KY 指出，
07/2026 KGD 營收表現仍受缺料影響 ， 環宇-KY 營收 1.45 億元 ，
MoM-55.41% ，不過，狀況自 08/2026 開始改善，將推升 PD 出
貨規模持續擴大，帶動 2026 年環宇 -KY 營收 YoY+48.22% 。  
此外，在 CW laser 的部份， 70mW CW laser 數款產品已通過
5,000 小時可靠性測試並小量試產，現階段環宇 -KY 則處審慎評
估承接量產訂單的階段，至於 100mW CW laser 目前已送樣驗
證中，預估最快 1Q27 小量試產。整體而言，群益預估 2H26 CW 
laser 營收比重仍偏低，惟長線成長想像空間大。

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就能源管理策略而言，首先，在提升能源使用效率的部份，環宇 -KY 一方面
積極使用 ERP 系統、電子郵件，並以電子化的方式減省來往信件、公函的
列印，以提升各項資源之使用效率、愛護地球資源；另一方面則於生產過程
中提高節能設備使用比重，以減少生產過程中電力的損耗，力求不浪費能
源。其次，在使用再生物料政策的部份，環宇 -KY 則針對生產製造過程中之
貴金屬廢棄物進行回收。  
就水資源、廢棄物管理策略而言，水資源管理或減量目標乃以減量目標為每
年減少 10% 的用水量；廢棄物管理政策或減量目標則關注環境並不斷改進
其流程以盡量減少浪費。

無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

5289.TT 宜鼎 
 需求仍然強勁且獲利結構穩定

[法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
宜鼎為工業級嵌入式儲存與記憶體解決方案領導廠商 。 公司深耕工業控制 、 航太國防 、
醫療及智慧交通等應用市場，產品以高可靠度、長生命週期及耐極端環境為標準，目
前全球客戶數超過 4,000 家，營運模式以高毛利、小量多樣的客製化服務為核心。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 2,080 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，1,780 
【大盤指數/股價】45975 / 1490

➢ 2Q26 受惠 Flash 報價上漲，帶動營收獲利成長 
2Q26 營收 224.43 億元，QoQ+70.3%，YoY+641.1%，推估 2Q26 
DRAM / SSD ASP 分別上漲 40% / 80%，出貨量則季增逾 10%。宜鼎
除了與原廠確保產能之外，同時也有增加供應商並簽訂 LTA，得以在需
求強勁下維持穩定庫存水位以及相對較低的庫存成本，故 2Q26 毛利率
65%，EPS 為 108.93 元，QoQ+89.7%，YoY+5,493.8%。  
➢ 3Q26 預期 ASP 仍具上漲動能 
展望 3Q26，預期 3Q26 DRAM/SSD 將分別季增 20% / 40%，出貨量
方面，預估 3Q26 出貨量將較 2Q26 呈現微幅季減。預估 3Q26 營收約
274.81 億元，QoQ+22.4%，YoY+621.8%。此外，受惠於貨源穩定，
加上大型客戶對於 DRAM 及 SSD 的終端需求表現依舊強勁，故宜鼎仍
可維持強勁獲利表現，預估 3Q26 毛利率約 65.5%，EPS 為 134 元，
QoQ+18.3%，YoY+1,808.1%。  
➢ 宜鼎貨源充足，預期獲利能力相對穩定 
考量 2H26 報價仍有望延續漲勢，加上網通與美、中系CSP 客戶拉貨動
能強勁，預估 2026 年營收約 923.89 億元 ，YoY+547.8%， 毛利率63.8%，
EPS 為 434.8 元，YoY+1,885.2%。預估 2027 年營收為 989.8 億元，
YoY+7.1%， 雖然仍預期DRAM/SSD 報價維持高檔不墜 ， 然而考量記憶
體供需缺口將進一步放大，恐使出貨量較 2026 年呈現收斂。預估 2027
年毛利率約為 57.2%，EPS 為 415.58 元，YoY-5.8%。 
➢ 看好記憶體趨勢不變，且網通、伺服器需求強勁，維持買進建議 
整體而言 ， 宜鼎受惠於記憶體報價上漲 ，2026 年營收 、 獲利皆大幅成長 ，
宜鼎不僅已向原廠簽訂 LTA 確保後續產能，且其客群多為網通、工控客
戶，較不容易因漲價而降低採購量，宜鼎具備議價能力。而 2027 年仍
預期記憶體供不應求態勢延續，安提的工廠自動化、機器人專案放量，
亦將優化整體獲利結構，同時挹注中長期營運動能。評價方面，考量宜
鼎貨源充足，且受惠客戶屬性，獲利結構相對穩定，再加上 2H26 報價
仍具上漲動能，維持買進建議，目標價 2,080 元(2027F PE 5X)。

個股報告

公司簡介 
宜鼎國際成立於 2005 年，為全球工業級嵌入式儲存與記憶體解決方案領導廠商。公
司長期深耕工業控制 、 航太國防 、 醫療及智慧交通等垂直應用市場 ， 產品以高可靠度 、
長生命週期及耐極端環境為核心標準，目前全球企業客戶數超過 4,000 家，營運模式
以高毛利 、 小量多樣的客製化服務為核心 。 公司透過旗下子公司安堤切入邊緣AI 運算
市場。安堤作為 NVIDIA Elite 級合作夥伴，專注於整合運算架構與 AI 軟體，母公司
則提供底層記憶體與儲存支持。此垂直整合模式使宜鼎從單純的零組件供應商，成功
轉型為具備軟硬體整合能力的 Edge AI 系統方案商」應用範疇已擴展至自動導引車
(AGV)、自主移動機器人及智慧監控等高附加價值領域。 
圖 1：宜鼎產品組合

資料來源：公司,國票投顧整理 
2Q26 受惠 Flash 報價上漲，帶動營收獲利成長 
2Q26 營收 224.43 億元，QoQ+70.3%，YoY+641.1%，主要仍受惠於記憶體供需失
衡導致報價持續上漲，且網通/工控客戶庫存水位相對較低，拉貨力道相對強勁，推估
2Q26 DRAM / SSD ASP 分別上漲 40%/80%，出貨量則季增逾 10%。獲利表現方面 ，
受惠於報價調漲，且 SSD 營收占比提升，再加上開機碟開始出貨予資料中心，整體產
品組合優化 ， 此外 ，宜鼎除了與原廠確保產能之外 ，同時也有增加供應商並簽訂 LTA，
得以在需求強勁下維持穩定庫存水位以及相對較低的庫存成本 ，故 2Q26 毛利率
65%，QoQ+5.9ppts，YoY+39.1ppts，稅後淨利 103.68 億元， QoQ+89.7% ，
YoY+5,493.8%，EPS 為 108.93 元。 
3Q26 預期 ASP 仍具上漲動能 
展望 3Q26，考量原廠持續漲價，預期 3Q26 DRAM / SSD 將分別季增 20% / 40%，
公司將逐月反映報價以轉嫁成本予客戶，出貨量方面，雖然網通、伺服器客戶訂單能
見度仍然強勁 ，惟工控客戶在記憶體大幅漲價， 以及CPU 缺貨的影響下，拉貨動能略
微放緩，故預估 3Q26 出貨量將較 2Q26 呈現微幅季減。預估 3Q26 營收約 274.81
億元 ，QoQ+22.4%，YoY+621.8%。此外，受惠於貨源穩定 ， 加上大型客戶對於DRAM
及 SSD 的終端需求表現依舊強勁，故宜鼎仍可維持強勁獲利表現，預估 3Q26 毛利
率約 65.5%，QoQ+0.5ppts，YoY+32.7ppts， 稅後淨利為122.7 億元 ，QoQ+18.3%，
YoY+1,808.1%，EPS 為 134 元。

個股報告 
宜鼎貨源充足，預期獲利能力相對穩定 
展望 2026 年，宜鼎將從單純的模組供應商轉型為 Edge AI 系統整合商。營運重心除
傳統工控外，美、中系CSP 客戶的邊緣運算專案將成為核心動能。從報價來看，預期
3Q26、4Q26 仍有望延續漲勢，加上網通與美、中系 CSP 客戶拉貨動能強勁，且子
公司安提的 AGV 與工廠自動化機器人等專案也已經自 1Q26 陸續開始出貨。投研部
預估 2026 年 DRAM 與 SSD 的出貨量皆可望年增逾 60%。此外，由於公司將持續向
客戶調漲報價以轉嫁成本，故 DRAM/SSD ASP 有望年增逾 200%。DRAM 產能方
面，宜鼎已經與韓廠確保全年穩定產能配貨，同時亦與台廠簽訂 LTA，以確保產能充
足；Flash 方面 ，宜鼎同樣與原廠談妥長期訂單 ， 確保部分利基型客戶在面臨產線EOL
時，仍有穩定供貨。而在客戶結構上，宜鼎亦成功切入美系存儲供應鏈，CSP 客戶營
收占比提升。此外，子公司安提在 Edge AI 專案已經邁入量產，營收自 2Q26 倍數成
長，預期 2026 年營收可望成長逾 200%。整體而言，預估 2026 年營收約 923.89 億
元，YoY+547.8%，毛利率 63.8%，YoY+32.7ppts，稅後淨利為 404.12 億元，
YoY+1,885.2%，EPS 為 434.8 元。 
投研部預估 2027 年營收為 989.8 億元，YoY+7.1%，雖然仍預期 DRAM / SSD 報價
維持高檔不墜，然而考量記憶體供需缺口將進一步放大， 恐使出貨量較 2026 年呈現
收斂；子公司安提方面，預期 2027 年仍將維持強勁成長動能，預期在新一代自動化
機器人專案量產貢獻下，營收可望年增逾 60%。獲利表現方面，考量公司產品終端應
用客戶多為工控、伺服器、網通等客戶，其價格敏感度相對較低，且預期拉貨動能仍
然強勁，再加上高毛利專案貢獻下，預期毛利率仍可維持相對高檔，預估 2027 年毛
利率約為 57.2%，YoY-6.6ppts，稅後淨利為 380.56 億元 ，YoY-5.8%，EPS 為 415.58
元。 
在產能規劃上，為了支持未來 Edge AI 市場的成長，公司規劃於宜蘭科學園區研發製
造中心興建三廠。預計於 4Q26 動工，並規劃在 2028 年完全落成啟用並投入產出。
在此之前，現有一廠與二廠的產能已預留充足空間，足以 支撐 2026 至 2027 年的營
運動能，確保宜鼎持續憑藉其軟硬體一站式整合的高附加價值與利基優勢，維持穩固
的地位。 
看好記憶體趨勢不變，且網通、伺服器需求強勁，維持買進建議 
整體而言 ，宜鼎受惠於記憶體報價上漲 ，2026 年營收 、 獲利皆大幅成長 ，且觀察SSD
需求隨著推論所需儲存空間不斷增加 ， 預計將維持供不應求態勢 ， 即便2027 年、2028
年原廠新增產能， 惟其大多將多數產能集中於DRAM，故短期內供需緊張態勢將難以
緩解。宜鼎不僅已向原廠簽訂 LTA 確保後續產能，且其客群多為網通、工控客戶，較
不容易因漲價而降低採購量，宜鼎具備議價能力，故即便庫存成本上升，仍預期宜鼎
毛利率仍可維持相對高檔。而 2027 年仍預期記憶體供不應求態勢延續，安提的工廠
自動化、機器人專案放量，亦將優化整體獲利結構，同時挹注中長期營運動能。評價
方面，過往本益比區間約為 3X~18X，目前本益比約為 3.6X，位於區間下緣，考量宜
鼎貨源充足 ， 且受惠客戶屬性 ， 獲利結構相對穩定 ， 再加上2H26 報價仍具上漲動能 ，
維持買進建議，目標價 2,080 元(2027F PE 5X)。

矽力*-KY (6415 TT)

上半年資料中心營收占比逾 15%

維持買進，目標價 558 元，潛在上漲空間 22% 
1. 生產成本上揚，2Q26 毛利率降至 47.0%，業外支撐 EPS 達 4.02 元。 
2. 主機板、eSSD 與光模組放量，上半年資料中心營收占比逾 15%。 
3. Gen4 預告訂單指引顯示，2027 年汽車、工業(含資料中心)成長擴大。

2Q26 毛利率降至 47.0%，業外支撐 EPS 達 4.02 元 
矽力*-KY 2Q26 合併營收 60.03 億元，QoQ+23.5%，YoY+31.5%，數字小幅優於
富邦及市場預期。產品組合方面，本季分別為消費(36%)、工業(35%)、資訊(9%)、
網通(6%)、 汽車(14%)，其中季成長以工業及汽車逾 50%最佳。 本季毛利率 47.0%，
較前季下滑 1.2 個百分點 ，主因生產成本上揚之影響， 數字低於預期。 業外淨利9.59
億元，絕大部分來自公允價值衡量之金融資產利益 9.59 億元。簡言之，矽力*-KY 
2Q26 營業利益 7.44 億元，稅前淨利 17.02 億元，歸屬母公司稅後淨利 15.71 億
元，EPS 4.02 元，數字優於富邦及市場預期。(以流通在外股本 9.77 億元，每股面
額 2.5 元、流通在外股數 3.908 億股計算，EPS 數字可能和 Cmoney 不同)

主機板、eSSD 與光模組放量，資料中心營收占比逾 15% 
管理層 8 月 28 日法說指出，Gen4 產品受惠 8 吋晶圓產能不足，客戶預告訂單強
勁，工業、汽車未來數季都會有不錯的成長。首先，工業項下的資料中心 2025 年
占比僅約中個位數，1H26 已上揚至 15%，預計 2027 年將達 20%以上。主要挹注
的產品線為 AI 伺服器主機板 、Enterprise SSD 電源供應與光模組等 。其次 ，vcore、
eFuse、信號鏈產品將自下半年開始在客戶端導入市場；電源數位控制器、電源晶
片、driver IC 等是未來兩年資料中心的成長趨動力。汽車方面，2026 年營收比重
預估可達 15-20%；2027 年在歐系客戶及新產品步入量產， 預期比重還會再成長。

下半年營收料逐季高升，全年 EPS 估達 12.12 元 
矽力*-KY 7 月營收 23.57 億元，MoM+13.1%，YoY+58.5%，主要受惠工業、汽
車急單挹注。管理層指引本季預期溫和成長，第四季為全年高峰。其次，生產成本
上揚將持續壓抑短期毛利率表現。因此，富邦預估矽力*-KY 3Q26 合併營收 67.48
億元 ，QoQ+12.4%，YoY+41.7%； 毛利率料大致持平在47.2%， 營業利益估10.46
億元，稅前淨利估 11.79 億元，EPS 估 2.74 元。至於 4Q26 預期營收、毛利率走
揚，EPS 估達 3.40 元，累積全年 EPS 估 12.12 元。

富邦不同之處 
我們對矽力*-KY FY2026-27 年 EPS 預估值分別為 12.12、17.65 元 ， 相較法說前同
業平均值分別約+20%、+1%。

評價與風險 
矽力*-KY 2Q26 毛利率低於預期 ，業外支撐 EPS 優於預期 。 管理層指出後市在 Gen4
產品逐步滲透，營收、毛利率可望漸次走高。公司目前股價約居 FY26 的 38XPER，
居歷史區間(24-72X)中下緣，評價不高，因此我們維持買進的投資評等，目標價設
定 558 元，約為 FY26 的 46.0XPER 或 FY27 的 31.6XPER。矽力*-KY 的投資風險
來自(1)資料中心對 PMIC 需求帶來的外溢效果不如預期；(2)新製程平台營收貢獻
低於管理層之預期等。

### Page 3

台灣股市 | 半導體  
公司速報

請參閱末頁之免責聲明 3

類比 IC 景氣加快回升，矽力-KY 具備產能優勢 
類比 IC 產業需求與價格回溫訊號明顯，主要趨動力來自 AI 基建與應用對電源產品
帶來產能排擠，推升晶圓前後段加工成本上揚。富邦認為此趨勢有利矽力*-KY 基本
面持續擴張 。 首先 ，8 吋晶圓產能緊缺有助矽力*-KY 的 12 吋 Gen4 放量速度加快 ，
目前客戶的預告訂單亦支持此看法。其次，儘管晶圓代工產能吃緊，矽力*-KY 除已
在去年提前下單生產晶圓，並已在 Gen4/Gen5 產品線備妥 2 家代工廠供客戶做選
擇；另外自建的封測產能亦有助緩解部分後段製程交期拉長的問題。簡言之，我們
看好類比 IC 產業正步入上行周期，矽力*-KY 基本面將持續受惠產業趨勢。

FY27：Gen4 佈局效益顯現、PMIC 定價環境改善 
展望 2027 年，HVDC 800V 導入資料中心最終將引起 PMIC 供給吃緊，有利改善
定價環境，推升毛利率重回正常區間。管理層 8 月 28 日法說指出 Gen4 的預告訂
單指引強勁，汽車新客戶明年開始放量 、 資料中心新產品亦持續擴大市占 ， 因此2027
年營收 YoY 成長目標仍為 2-3 成。簡言之，我們預估矽力*-KY 2027 年合併營收達
315.83 億元，YoY+26.7%，毛利率約 49.6%，營業利益估 71.83 億元，稅後淨利
估 68.99 億元，全年 EPS 估 17.65 元。

富邦的股票評等標準 
評等  定義 
買進 預估未來6個月內的絕對報酬超過15%  
中立  預估未來6個月內有絕對報酬介於15%與負15%之間 
賣出 預估未來6個月內的絕對報酬高於負15% 
未評等 由於富邦目前與該公司有特定交易或沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等  
評估中 目前正在研議個股的投資評等，將於3到6個月內提供投資評等

產業評等 定義 
優於大盤 預估該產業在未來6個月內會比大盤指數表現突出 
持平 預估該產業在未來6個月內與大盤指數表現相較持平 
劣於大盤 預估該產業在未來6個月內會比大盤指數表現較差

This report has been produced in Chinese. In the event of questions regarding the accuracy of the translation, please refer t o the original Chinese version.

Fubon's Rating System 
Rating Definition 
Buy Expected absolute return to be over 15% within the next 6 months  
Neutral  Expected absolute return to be level within the next 6 months  
Sell  Expected absolute return to be over negative 15% within the next 6 months 
Not Rated (NR)  Pursuant to Fubon acting in deals involving the company or there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an investment rating  
Under Review Fubon is in the process of determining an investment rating and will assign a rating within the 3- to 6-month horizon

Sector Rating Definition 
Overweight Sector expected to outperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months 
Market Weight  Sector expected to perform in line with the broader market averages within the next 6-12 months 
Underweight  Sector expected to underperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months.

- 1 - 
個股報告 
鈺邦(6449) 
AI 需求帶動 CAP 供不應求，擴產與漲價支撐 2027年獲利 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $365 
前次投資建議：2025.12.23 
「逢低買進」 ，目標價: $193

報告日期：2026.08.28 研 究 員：李彝安

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ AI Server已成鈺邦最大應用 ， 高規CAP需求持續高於現有產能 ，27H1月產能將由目前 7,000
萬顆增至 1.2 億顆；配合 26H2 雙位數漲價、高規產品出貨增加及 SMLC 新應用導入，預估
2027 年 EPS 15.19元、上修目標價至 365元，潛在漲幅 56.7%，評等上調為「買進」。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/08/27 收盤價： 233.0 ➢ 26Q2 營收 13.7 億元，季增 21.4%、年增 20.4%，創
單季新高；毛利率 34.4%，季減 2.4 個百分點，主要受
原物料成本上漲影響，EPS 2.31元。7月營收 5.06億
元、年增34.8%，再創單月新高。原物料成本上升已啟
動約雙位數漲價，客戶陸續接受， 26H2 開始反映於毛
利。 
➢ 目前 B/B Ratio 大於 1， 整體需求高於現有供給約30%，CAP
為主要產能瓶頸。CAP月產能約 7,000萬顆，預計2026 年
11月增至 8,000 萬顆、2027 年 1 月達 1 億顆，27H1 進一
步提升至 1.2 億顆，較目前增加約 70%；高容 MLCC 供給
吃緊亦增加 CAP 與 MLCC搭配使用及部分替代機會。 
➢ Hybrid可滿足 AI Server 高功率電源需求，目前為公司毛利
率最高產品；SMLC 則鎖定部分鉭質電容替代市場，目前
NB 已導入出貨，企業級 SSD 仍處認證階段。2026 年資本
支出約 10 億元，2027 年預估降至 6至 8 億元，投資重心由
V-Chip轉向 CAP 及 SMLC。 
➢ 研究部預估 2026 年營收 57.6 億元、年增 28.7%，毛利率
36.3%、年增1.6個百分點， EPS 10.14元；2027年隨 CAP
新增產能持續開出、AI Server 高規產品需求增加及產品組
合改善，預估營收81.1億元、年增40.9%，毛利率38.0%、
年增 1.7 個百分點，EPS 15.19 元。目前 CAP 需求仍高於
現有供給，且27H1 月產能將較目前增加約 70%，支撐2027
年營收及獲利維持高成長；給予24× 27F PER，目標價 365
元，潛在上漲空間 56.7%，投資評等為「買進」 。

營 運 分 析 
鈺邦為全球主要固態電容廠，產品重心由 PC應用轉 AI Server高規 CAP及
Hybrid 
鈺邦主要從事導電性高分子固態電容研發、生產及銷售，主要產品包括插件
型 DIP、捲繞型 V-Chip、晶片型 CAP、Hybrid 及 SMLC。捲繞型固態電容
產能及出貨量居全球第一，晶片型產品規模居全球第二；生產據點涵蓋台灣、
中國無錫、湖北及泰國，並由台灣子公司旭積科技布局 SMLC。 
公司產品應用由傳統 PC逐步向 AI Server、 高階NB及網通移動 ， 其中CAP、
V-Chip及 Hybrid 均已應用於 Server，SMLC則持續拓展 NB、Server 及 SSD
等市場。2025 年營收 44.7 億元，近年營運成長主要受惠高規固態電容需求
增加。

圖一、鈺邦(6449) 2026Q2產品營收比重

資料來源：鈺邦、兆豐投顧彙整

26Q2 營收創高，原物料成本上升壓抑毛利率 
26Q2 營收 13.7 億元 ， 季增21.4%、 年增20.4%， 創單季新高 ； 毛利率34.4%，
季減 2.4 個百分點、年減 2.1 個百分點，營業利益 2.2 億元，歸屬母公司淨
利 2.0億元，EPS 2.31元。毛利率下滑主要受到原物料成本上漲影響，並非

- 3 - 
個股報告 
終端需求轉弱；26Q2 CAP營收占比升至 48%，V-Chip及 DIP分別為 33%、
19%，Server 應用占比則提高至 37.9%，高規產品出貨仍維持成長。 
7 月營收 5.06 億元，月增 9.2%、年增 34.8%，再創單月歷史新高，26Q3
營收動能延續。因應原物料價格上漲，產品已啟動約雙位數價格調整，且客
戶陸續接受，新價格自 26H2 開始反映；預估 26Q3 營收 15.6 億元、季增
13.6%，毛利率回升至 36.6%，EPS 2.84 元，26Q4 營收進一步增至 17.03
億元，毛利率 37.2%，EPS 3.05元。

AI Server升至第一大應用，CAP成為主要成長產品 
26H1 Server 營收年增 228.9%、占比 35.6%，已成鈺邦第一大應用；NB占
比 27.5%、營收年增 27.3%。產品端則以 CAP成長最明顯，26H1 營收年增
77.3%，V-Chip亦年增 34.2%。相較傳統 PC 相關需求偏弱，Server 高規產
品持續放量，已成目前營收成長主要來源。 
CAP同時受惠 AI Server 及 NB 新規格導入，26Q2 營收較前季增加約 50%，
且已成最大產品線。AI Server 功率持續提升，帶動系統所需電容量增加，
CAP、V-Chip 及 Hybrid 皆受惠規格升級；其中 Hybrid 可滿足高規 Server
電源需求，目前亦為鈺邦毛利率最高產品。研究部預期 2027 年營收成長仍
以 CAP 放量為主，V-Chip 及 Hybrid 則隨 Server 需求增加持續貢獻，同時
27H2 SMLC貢獻於估將更為顯著。

CAP需求高於供給，27H1月產能增至 1.2億顆 
目前公司 B/B Ratio維持>1，整體需求高於現有供給約 30%，CAP成長主要
限制已由訂單轉為產能。CAP 月產能目前約 70M，預計 2026 年 11月提升
至 80M、2027 年 1月達 100M，27H1 進一步增至 120M， 較目前增加約70%。
2026 年資本支出約 10 億元，2027 年預估降至 6 至 8 億元，主要因 V-Chip
擴產逐步告一段落，後續投資轉向 CAP及 SMLC。

客戶端目前以合作逾十年的兩家美系業者為主，近期另新增一家日系客戶，
而本波產能擴充主要仍來自既有客戶需求增加。AI光通訊供應鏈需求維持強
勁，目前 COSA產能持續滿載，訂單能見度已延伸至 2027 年底。研究部預
估此一趨勢將延續至 2027 年，並成為營收與毛利率同步成長的主要來源。

26H2漲價效益反映，高規產品帶動毛利率回升 
原物料成本上漲造成 26Q2 毛利率降至 34.4%，產品已啟動約雙位數價格調
整，預期 26H2毛利率將高於 26Q1 毛利率。配合 CAP、V-Chip及 Hybrid
高規產品出貨增加，研究部預估毛利率由 26Q3 的 36.6%提升至 26Q4 的
37.2%，2026 年全年毛利率 36.3%、年增 1.6 個百分點。 
2027 年除 26H2 漲價完整反映全年外，CAP新增產能逐步開出且目前需求
仍高於供給，有利新增設備稼動率；同時AI Server占比提升帶動高規 CAP、
V-Chip及 Hybrid出貨 ， 其中Hybrid為目前毛利率最高產品 。 研究部預估2027
年毛利率進一步升至 38.0%、年增 1.7 個百分點。

- 4 - 
個股報告

SMLC 切入 NB，SSD認證提供後續成長空間 
SMLC目前已切入 NB 應用並開始出貨，企業級SSD仍處客戶認證階段，短
期營收貢獻仍低於 CAP、V-Chip 及 Hybrid。2027 年資本支出規劃亦納入
SMLC產能，顯示產品已由前期開發逐步進入量產及市場拓展階段。 
研究部 2027 年預估仍以 CAP擴產及 AI Server 高規產品成長為主要假設，
尚未納入 SMLC 大幅放量。後續若企業級 SSD 認證完成並進入量產，將增
加現有預估之外的營收來源，因此 SMLC 現階段較適合作為 2027 年後續上
修項目，而非 2026 年主要獲利驅動。

圖二、鈺邦(6449)各季營收及獲利走勢預估

資料來源：Cmoney；兆豐投顧預估

CAP擴產與毛利率提升，2027年 EPS估 15.19元  
研究部預估 2026 年營收 57.6 億元、年增 28.7%，毛利率 36.3%、年增 1.6
個百分點，營業利益 10.9 億元，歸屬母公司淨利 9.3 億元、年增 43.6%，
EPS 10.14 元。26H2 隨 CAP產能增加、AI Server 高規產品出貨成長及漲
價效益開始反映，預估 26Q3、26Q4 營收分別 15.6 億元、17.0 億元，毛利
率 36.6%、37.2%，獲利逐季提升。 
2027 年 CAP 月產能由目前 7,000 萬顆逐步提升至 27H1 的 1.2 億顆，新增
產能持續貢獻，配合 AI Server 高規產品需求增加及產品組合改善，研究部
預估營收 81.1 億元、年增40.9%，毛利率 38.0%、年增 1.7 個百分點，營業
利益 17.7 億元，歸屬母公司淨利 14.0 億元、年增 49.9%，EPS 15.19 元。
獲利增幅高於營收，主要反映營收規模放大及毛利率提升。 
考量 CAP 需求仍高於現有供給，且 27H1 月產能較目前增加約 70%，2027
年營收及獲利成長具明確產能支撐；同時 26H2 漲價效益完整反映全年，高

1 
6609 瀧澤科(TAKISAWA)

報告日期： 2026/09/02 研 究 員：洪  國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 公告 2Q26 稅後 EPS 0.28 元 
瀧澤科 2Q26 營收金額 6.63 億元， YoY+1.07% ，QoQ+34.88% ，
營業利益 0.30 億元， YoY+2.89% ，較 1Q26 由虧轉盈，稅後盈餘
0.20 億元，較 2Q25 與 1Q26 由虧轉盈 ，稅後EPS 0.28 元。1H26
稅後 EPS 0.12 元，優於去年同期 -0.74 元。

二. 預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 0.56 元 
瀧澤科 07/2026 營收金額 2.39 億元，YoY+62.79% ，MoM-2.3% 。 
在手訂單己有小幅回升，宏遠預估瀧澤科 3Q26 營收金額 6.99 億
元，YoY+81.06% ，QoQ+5.4% ， 預估營業利益0.33 億 元，較3Q25
由虧轉盈 ，QoQ+8.2% ， 預估稅後盈餘0.40 億元 ，YoY+867.26% ，
QoQ+97.36% ，預估稅後 EPS 0.56 元。

三. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 1.10 元 
瀧澤科 2026 年在日本瀧澤在母公司日本 Nidec 集團資源挹注 ， 帶
動 CNC 車床與 OEM 車床同步成長，推升營運走高。宏遠預估瀧
澤科 2026 年營收金額 25.00 億元， YoY+22.64% ，預估營業利益
0.70 億元，由虧轉盈，預估稅後盈餘 0.80 億元，由虧轉盈，預估
稅後 EPS 1.10 元。

四. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 1.80 元 
瀧澤科 2027 年在 Nidec 集團效益持續始發酵 ， 加上透過集團銷售
平台與代理商，逐步將產品推到集團的供應商與客戶，其中瀧澤
科己接到集團內的水冷散熱加工設備訂單，有助整體出貨量持續
成長，推升 2027 年營運走高。宏遠預估瀧澤科 2027 年營收金額
27.50 億元， YoY+10.00% ，預估營業利益 1.44 億元，
YoY+105.18% ，預估稅後盈餘 1.30 億元， YoY+63.46% ，預估稅
後 EPS 1.80 元。

五. 投資建議買進，目標價 50 元 
觀察瀧澤科歷史 PER 6~17X ，台灣瀧澤科在日本 Nidec 集團資源
整合效率發酵 ， 己成功切入水冷散熱設備供應鏈 ， 有助評價提升，
故投資建議買進，目標價 50 元（ 28X 2027 年稅後 EPS ）。

2 
貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
瀧澤科成立於 1971 年，資本額 7.25 億元，瀧澤科為日本瀧澤科
持股 51% 子公司（日本瀧澤科己於 2023 年被 NIDEC 收購下市）。
主要是以生產 CNC 車床為主， 1998 年開始生產 PCB 鑽孔機，瀧
澤科主要以 CNC 車床 、PCB 鑽孔機與替日本瀧澤科代工生產 CNC
車床為營收三大來源。 CNC 車床主要應用於汽機車模具零組件加
工、國防航太與其他各種機器類零件加工為主，其中以汽車模具
業占比最大，約占 40~50% 。營收地區方面，亞洲地區 60~61% 、
歐洲比重 16~17% 、美洲地區比重 12~13% 、台灣內銷 9~10% 。

二. 2026 年營運概況及展望

預估 2026 年 CNC 車床營收金額 19.00 億元， YoY+15.1%

瀧澤科 CNC 車床主要用於機車、汽車、自行車零件加工、國防航
太工業的零件加工，屬切削工具機。瀧澤科 2025 年 CNC 車床出
貨量 998 台， YoY-11.99% ，貢獻 2025 年營收金額 16.51 億元，
YoY-17.1%。 營收衰退主因在於受到對等關稅 ， 導致全球經濟未見
明顯復甦回溫，企業資本支出縮減所致。

母公司日本瀧澤己被日本 Nidec 併購 ，Nidec 為專業馬達製造廠，
生產電動車及電子馬達，基於對工具機有需求，相關客戶也需要
用到工具機，過去三年公開收購日本 OKK 及三菱、義大利 PAMA
三家工具機廠，預期在集團資源整合，將有助台灣瀧澤 CNC 訂單
增加，預估瀧澤科 CNC 車床 2026 年出貨量 1100 台，
YoY+10.22% ，貢獻 2026 年營收金額 19.00 億元， YoY+15.1% 。

預期 2027 年，在 Nidec 集團資源持續整合 ， 將有助於瀧澤科CNC
車床出貨持續增加，另外在中國大陸機器人產業發展熱絡帶動
下，瀧澤科己接獲中國大陸減速機與馬達公司訂單，預計 2027 年
開始正式出貨，預估瀧澤科 CNC 車床 2027 年出貨量 1180 台，
YoY+7.27% ，貢獻 2027 年營收金額 20.9 億元， YoY+100.9% 。

預估 2026 年 OEM 車床營收金額 4.50 億元， YoY+124.9%

OEM 車床方面，瀧澤科為日本母公司株式瀧澤 OEM 代工。瀧澤
科 2025 年 OEM 車床出貨量 444 台，YoY-54.74% ，貢獻 2025 年
營收金額 2.45 億元， YoY-54.5% ，營收衰退主因在於日本瀧澤
2025 年受到美國關稅影響 ， 訂單大幅減少 ， 間接影響OEM 訂單。

林秋香 
886-2-27815995#37026 
mandy.cs.lin@fubon.com 
兆聯實業 (6944 TT)

高科技產業擴廠帶動公司營運續揚

投資評等維持買進，目標價 976 元，潛在漲幅 37% 
1. 毛利率跳升帶動 2Q26 獲利優於預期 
2. 在手訂單續創新高 
3. 因應國內外需求，持續擴增產能將推升營運成長

毛利率跳升帶動 2Q26 獲利優於預期 
兆聯實業 2Q26 營收為 50.8 億元，YoY+36.1%，略低於預期。其中工程類營收為
39.3 億元(YoY+35.0%)，占比 77%，主要係因在手工程持續去化；服務類營收為
11.5 億元(YoY+40.3%)， 成長轉趨強勁主要係因2H25 開始有一個新的製程使用新
的耗材所致，占比 23%。受惠於 1)美國專案工程執行效率佳，完工進度較預期時間
短、2)新承做專案因業主急於擴廠，對施工進度的考量甚於成本，故公司的接單毛
利率較高，與 3)去年接單時台幣較為強勢，今年台幣趨貶，故 2Q26 工程毛利率上
揚至 28%( vs. 1Q26 的 26.5%)，服務類毛利率則達 30.1% (vs. 1Q26 的 29.9%)，
帶動整體毛利率達 28.4%，優於預期。因此，稅後淨利達 8.7 億元，YoY+70.8%，
亦高於研究部預期。若以除權後股本計算，EPS 為 8.75 元。

在手訂單續創新高 
受惠於 AI 需求強勁，半導體、記憶體、PCB、ABF 產業建廠積極度高，兆聯實業 6
月底在手訂單餘額達 277 億元，分別為國內 73%、美國 25%、新加坡 7%、中國
<5%。公司並於 7 月發布重訊公告接獲美國 50 億元以上訂單，若含此訂單，美國
占比將提升至 40%。 由於在手訂單續創新高 ， 研究部正向看待未來工程類營收成長 ，
並預估 3Q26 營收為 59.6 億元，YoY+24.5%。基於 3Q26 工程類毛利率可望持平
於 2Q26 之假設，預估稅後淨利為 10.4 億元，YoY+51.9%，EPS 為 10.4 元。

因應國內外需求，持續擴增產能將推升營運成長 
兆聯實業近期公告將發行兩檔無擔保轉換公司債，合計金額為新台幣 25 億元，主
要用於支應美國營運資金需求 ， 預估此對股權稀釋比例約3%。 因應客戶建廠需求 ，
為取得資本財與服務財優勢，公司於台灣中南部有購置土地或建廠計畫，資本支出
約 10 億元；海外方面，公司將投入 19.5 億元建置美國新廠(第一期)，此廠可望在
2028 年完工。雖然業主隨著發包案量提升而有培養其他供應商的計畫，然因兆聯
實業已與業主合作多年，建廠配合默契佳，與國際競爭者相較亦 有成本費用優勢，
故公司競爭性仍佳。除了優化工程進度以提升毛利率外，公司目前已切入記憶體、
先進封裝、載板的純水系統，因純水系統的毛利率較高，未來若能切入半導體製造
廠的純水系統，則毛利率將再提升，另外，美國新廠完成後，公司服務類營收將加
速成長，毛利率亦將隨之提升。

富邦不同之處 
富邦 2026/2027 EPS(F) 為 38.54/48.79 元，Bloomberg 預估為 37.74/41.11 元。

評價與風險 
兆聯實業聚焦在高科技廠水資源工程，因半導體大客戶先進製程擴展不停歇，將帶
動業績再創新高。評價風險為半導體產業擴張放緩，以及原料與人工成本飆漲。

1 
7689大鵬科(Climax Co.)

報告日期： 2026/09/03 研 究 員：方姿婷  (Lesley Fang)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 大鵬科為安防無線通訊整體解決方案之專業廠商，以
自主研發之 F1 系列無線通訊技術平台為核心，深耕安防
與長照無線通訊利基市場多年，產品 組合具技術差異化
優勢，營收規模穩中求變化  
大鵬科技為無線安防與長照警報設備系統製造商，總部位於台北內
湖，生產基地為台灣台北內湖及汐止。大鵬科技營運主要專注於無
線通訊安防、長照警報系統之研發、製造與銷售。以 ODM 模式，主
要客戶為安防服務提供商，供應產品為安防警報系統、智慧家庭安
全設備、長者遠距智能照護居家設備，深耕歐洲、北美、中南美洲
與亞洲市場，大鵬科技營收比重安防警報系統佔 75%、長照警報系
統佔 2 5 %，大鵬科技銷售區域比重，歐洲佔 6 1 . 8 4 %、南美佔
19.76%、北美佔 14.24%、其他區域佔 4.16%。展望 2H26，儘管 2H26
當前經營層面，面臨電子產品記憶體價格上漲、封測晶圓代工吃
緊、PCB 板(印刷電路板)與 PCB 原材料缺料價漲等，各方面零組件
價格居高不下，宏遠預估公司仍可守住一定程度的產品毛利率，皆
源自於大鵬科技優越之成本管控與產品組合優化能力。

二. 宏遠預估 2026 年安防警報產品營收雙位數成長，毛利
率穩健增長 
隨未來智能家居的普及，以及人們安全意識的提升，家庭安防市場
的需求持續增長。此外，全球老齡化趨勢也推動了對長者遠距智能
照護居家設備的需求上升。大鵬憑藉安全、高效、省電、可靠的無
線通訊技術，及高度客製化的靈活性以及經驗豐富的研發團隊，能
快速回應全球不同地區客戶的需求，在市場中建立強大的競爭優
勢。大鵬科技旗下產品，挾帶自主研發窄頻無線通訊技術，藉此延
伸出 F1/SF1/SFV 無線通訊技術架構，於此多協定通訊技術的自主研
發可以在短期間內滿足客戶需求，具備完整之硬體與軟韌體完整之
解決方案。例如：大鵬科技之安防與長照產品，影像傳輸速度與時
間優於同業，大鵬科技安防產品電池壽命為同業之一倍以上，可供
傳輸距離也為同業產品之三倍以上，大鵬科技旗下產品主訴傳輸技
術(低功耗、長距離大於 2 公里以上傳輸距離、高穿透性、高抗干擾
等技術)均為大鵬科技領先同業之競爭優勢。大鵬科技自主研發之窄
頻無線通訊技術，為大鵬科技提供更多元之軟韌體、硬體整合平台
與市場發展空間。日前，大鵬科技已於台北市內湖購置新土地與廠
投資評等：買進 
目標價位：230.00 元

房建物建置，新廠預計於 2029 年投產，投產後預估可擴充約 55~60%
產能，顯現其長期業績成長趨勢明確。

三. 2026 年起邊緣 AI 新品加入，產能同步擴充營運增長 
宏遠投顧認為，大鵬科技在技術創新投入不遺餘力，2009 年至 2024
之間，2009 年起大鵬科技即加入 ZigBee、Z-Wave 等國際聯盟， 進
軍智慧家庭市場；2012 年推出首款安防警報 SaaS 平台，其後陸續成
功開發自有專屬的 F1、SF1、SFV 無線通訊技術，自行研發之通訊
技術與規格構築大鵬科技技術護城河。大鵬科技最大的技術壁壘在
於其自主研發之「F1 系列無線通訊技術平台(F1/SF1/SFV)」 ，與國際
大廠相較之下，因為大鵬科技提供較為省電、傳輸距離較遠以及傳
輸效率較高，客戶傾向採用自有的私有通訊協定(F1、SF1、SFV 無
線通訊技術)，而不愛用傳輸容易受到干擾的通用 Wi-Fi 或 Bluetooth
傳輸協定，轉而向大鵬科技下訂單。隨著邊緣 AI 落地，智慧安防概
念提升，樓宇自動化商機即將來臨，企業園區、商辦大樓、社區、
工地與公共場域對安全管理需求不斷提高，科技系統保全正從單點
設備監控，逐步升級為結合 AI、機器人、無人機與雲端平台的智慧
化服務。以大鵬科技產品為例，主打無線傳輸之安全監控產品，透
過即時影像分析與權限辨識，協助場域進行異常偵測、人員辨識與
安全管理，經由大鵬科技自行開發之演算法(Algorithm)直接內建於
終端晶片，引入深度學習與邊緣運算技術，智慧安防將引領安防產
品從過去的「事後舉證(被動式錄影)」演進為「事前預警、即時處置
(主動防禦架構)」，並且，大鵬科技之無線傳輸安全監控產品，可為
客戶實現更低功耗、省電節能之功效，相較於同業提供有線安防產
品，大鵬科技無線產品將具備市場競爭力。

四.投資建議：買進，目標價 230 元 
大鵬科技公司競爭優勢為，主要專注於無線通訊安防、長照警報系
統之研發、製造與銷售。以 ODM 模式，供應產品為安防警報系統、
智慧家庭安全設備、長者遠距智能照護居家設備，深耕歐洲、北
美、中南美洲與亞洲市場，深耕安防領域多年，且足以提供值得信
賴之各項產品，對於公司長期營運挹注穩健成長動能。展望 2026
年，預估大鵬科技 2026 年營收為 54.98 億元，+23.25%YoY，毛利率
為 30.67%，稅後淨利為 6.95 億元，稅後 EPS+11.88 元。展望 2027
年，預估大鵬科技 2027 年營收為 63.66 億元，+15.79%YoY，毛利率
30.77%，稅後淨利 8.50 億元，稅後 EPS+14.53 元(以 5.85 億元股本為
基礎計)。市場期待，智慧安全監控與居家保全廠商受惠於家用終端
產品線與邊緣 AI 之滲透率逐步提升，帶動營運步入成長軌道，此
外，由於歐洲 EN 54 火災偵測與警報系統相關標準，隨相關規範更
新及各國當地法規陸續跟進開放，火災偵測與警報系統之市場，開
